[01514] School of Specialization for Legal Professions
INFO General information for the current academic year Duration: 2 years. Established: 1972. Status: Open. Credits: 2 CFU. Language: Italian. Type: Specialization Schools. Regulations: Pre-Reform School of Specialization for Legal Professions. Inter-university course: No. Open access: Yes. Location: UNIVERSITY OF SALERNO - FISCIANO. Department: DEPARTMENT OF LEGAL SCIENCES (SCHOOL OF JURISPRUDENCE). Website: HTTP://WEB.UNISA.IT/DIDATTICA/SCUOLE-SPECIALIZZAZIONE/SCUOLE-SPECIALIZZAZIONE. Verification of required knowledge for access: No. Course lecturers: View list. Admission requirements: Mandatory qualifications: [TSS] High school diploma. Optional qualifications (choose from the following): [L1+TSS] Bachelor's degree, [LS+TSS] Specialist degree, [LM+TSS] Master's degree. Course Coordinator: LAURA SOLIDORO. School Director: GIOVANNI SCIANCALEPORE. Chairman of the didactic council: LAURA SOLIDORO.

UNIVERSITY: University of Salerno
COURSE: [03126] LITERATURE
TYPE: Bachelor's Degree
SECTION: General Description
URL: https://unisa.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/500165

[03126] LITERATURE BACHELOR'S DEGREE
COURSE AND TEACHING INFORMATION General information relating to the current academic year. Duration: 3 years. Admission: 2023. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Type: Three-year degree. Regulations: D.M. 270/2004. Degree class: L-10 - Class of degrees in Literature. Inter-university course: No. Admission test: Self-assessment test. Location: UNIVERSITY OF SALERNO - FISCIANO. Department: DEPARTMENT OF HUMANISTIC STUDIES. Verification of knowledge required for admission: No. Course teachers: View list. Admission requirements: Optional qualifications (to be chosen from the following): [TSS-CONS], Upper secondary school diploma [TS], Foreign qualification. Persons responsible for approving study plans: ROSA GIULIO, MARINA POLITO, STEFANO GRAZZINI, SILVIA SINISCALCHI, MASSIMO LAZZERI, SABRINA GALANO, MARIO D'AMBROSI, CAROLINA STROMBOLI, GIORGIO SICA, PIERLUIGI DE FELICE, GIANPAOLO URSO. Student mobility responsibility: STEFANO GRAZZINI. Chairman of the didactic council: STEFANO GRAZZINI. Characteristics and other information: Description of the study program: STUDY PLAN. Access to further studies: - ENROLLMENT IN A MASTER'S DEGREE PROGRAM - FIRST-LEVEL MASTER. Professional status conferred by the degree: - OPERATOR FOR CULTURAL AND EDUCATIONAL SERVICES - EXPERT IN LITERARY, LINGUISTIC AND HISTORICAL SUBJECTS - OPERATOR IN EDITORIAL AND EDITING PROCESSES - COMMUNICATIONS OFFICER. Official language(s) of teaching and assessment: ITALIAN. Specific learning objectives: THE COURSE AIMS TO TRAIN PROFESSIONALS WITH BASIC KNOWLEDGE IN THE LINGUISTIC, PHILOLOGICAL-LITERARY, HISTORICAL, GEOGRAPHICAL, PHILOSOPHICAL AND HISTORICAL-ARTISTIC FIELDS OF ANTIQUITY, THE MIDDLE AGES, THE MODERN AND CONTEMPORARY ERAS. CULTURAL OPERATORS WHO CAN APPLY THESE SKILLS IN PUBLIC AND PRIVATE ORGANIZATIONS, IN THE FIELD OF HUMAN RESOURCES, JOURNALISM, COMMUNICATION, AND EDUCATION. Dublin descriptors: I - Knowledge and understanding: GRADUATES IN LITERATURE MUST POSSESS: • A SOLID BASIC TRAINING, BASED ON A CORRECT METHODOLOGY IN SYNCHRONY AND DIACHRONY, IN LITERARY, LINGUISTIC, HISTORICAL, AND PHILOLOGICAL STUDIES, ACQUIRED THROUGH THE STUDY OF ADVANCED TEXTBOOKS AND ORIGINAL SOURCES; • ESSENTIAL KNOWLEDGE OF THE LITERARY, LINGUISTIC, HISTORICAL, PHILOSOPHICAL, GEOGRAPHICAL, AND ARTISTIC CULTURE OF ANTIQUITY, THE MIDDLE AGES, THE MODERN AND CONTEMPORARY ERAS, WITH DIRECT EXPERIENCE OF TEXTS AND DOCUMENTS; • INTERMEDIATE KNOWLEDGE OF AT LEAST ONE MODERN FOREIGN LANGUAGE. THE TRANSFER OF THIS KNOWLEDGE IS CARRIED OUT THROUGH LECTURES, EXERCISES, INTERIM ASSESSMENTS, LABORATORIES, AND SEMINARS. II - Ability to apply knowledge and understanding: THE LITERATURE GRADUATE: • COLLECTS, CLASSIFIES, AND SYNTHESIZES HISTORICAL, LITERARY, AND ARTISTIC DATA AND INFORMATION, USING SCIENTIFIC BIBLIOGRAPHY; • ANALYZES TEXTS FROM THE ORIGINAL, TRANSLATING AND INTERPRETING THEM CRITICALLY. Level of satisfaction of students and employment status of graduates: PROFILE OF GRADUATES AND EMPLOYMENT STATUS III - Autonomy of judgment: GRADUATES OF THE COURSE SHOULD HAVE DEVELOPED THE ABILITY TO ANALYZE AND FILTER DATA AUTONOMOUSLY, DEVELOPING CRITICAL CONSIDERATIONS. AUTONOMY OF JUDGMENT WILL BE TESTED BOTH IN THE EXAM TESTS AND THROUGH THE FINAL EXAM, IN WHICH THE GRADUATE CAN FULLY EXPRESS THE ACQUISITION OF THEIR OWN SYSTEM OF EVALUATION AND ELABORATION WITHIN SPECIFIC CULTURAL DOMAINS. IV - Communication skills: COMMUNICATION SKILLS WILL CONSIST OF THE ABILITY TO ADAPT TO THE DIFFERENT COMMUNICATION LEVELS OF A CONTEXT OR WORK GROUP, COLLABORATING EFFECTIVELY AND INTERACTING IN A PRECISE AND CLEAR MANNER EVEN WITH NON-SPECIALISTS IN THE FIELD, THROUGH KNOWLEDGE OF AT LEAST ONE FOREIGN LANGUAGE (LEVEL B1). V - Learning ability: THE LITERATURE GRADUATE MUST DEMONSTRATE THAT THEY HAVE ACQUIRED OR DEVELOPED: • AN EFFECTIVE LEARNING TECHNIQUE; • AN ABILITY TO CRITICAL ANALYSIS OF INFORMATION; • A RATIONAL ORGANIZATION OF STUDY TIME; • THE FUNDAMENTAL PRINCIPLES OF THE SCIENTIFIC METHOD APPLIED TO LITERARY DISCIPLINES; • FAMILIARITY WITH ALL THE BASIC TOOLS OF RESEARCH THAT ALLOW THEM TO QUICKLY FIND BIBLIOGRAPHICAL INFORMATION.

-----

UNIVERSITY: University of Salerno
COURSE: [03221] MODERN PHILOLOGY
TYPE: Master's Degree Course
SECTION: General Description
URL: https://unisa.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/500168

[03221] MODERN PHILOLOGY MASTER'S DEGREE COURSE
COURSE STRUCTURE AND COURSES Information regarding the current academic year Duration: 2 years. Admission year: 2019. Status: Open. Credits: 120 CFU. Language: Italian. Type: Master's Degree. Regulations: D.M. 270/2004. Degree class: LM-14 - Master's Degree in Modern Philology. Inter-university course: No. Admission: Self-assessment test. Location: UNIVERSITY OF SALERNO - FISCIANO. Department: DEPARTMENT OF HUMANISTIC STUDIES. Verification of knowledge required for admission: No. Course lecturers: See list. Admission requirements: Optional qualifications (choose from the following): [TSS+L2], First-level degree, Upper secondary school diploma [TS], Foreign qualification [L1], Upper secondary school diploma, Degree. Approval of study plans: ROSA GIULIO, MARINA POLITO, STEFANO GRAZZINI, SILVIA SINISCALCHI, MASSIMO LAZZERI, SABRINA GALANO, MARIO D'AMBROSI, CAROLINA STROMBOLI, GIORGIO SICA, PIERLUIGI DE FELICE, GIANPAOLO URSO. Student mobility: STEFANO GRAZZINI. Head of the teaching council: STEFANO GRAZZINI. Characteristics and other information: Description of the course: STUDY PLAN. Access to further studies: SECOND-LEVEL MASTER'S DEGREES, SPECIALIZATION SCHOOLS, RESEARCH DOCTORATES. Professional status conferred by the degree: EXPERT IN THE DESIGN OF DIGITAL LEARNING PATHWAYS, CURATORS, AND CULTURAL OPERATORS/CONSULTANTS, SPECIALISTS IN THE DESIGN AND EXPERIMENTATION OF RESEARCH. Official language(s) of teaching and assessment: ITALIAN. Specific learning objectives: THE MASTER'S DEGREE IN MODERN PHILOLOGY AIMS TO TRAIN INTELLECTUALS WHO, BASED ON ADVANCED SCIENTIFIC AND DISCIPLINARY KNOWLEDGE, TRANSVERSAL COMPUTER SKILLS, AND HIGH ANALYTICAL CAPABILITIES, CAN DEVELOP ADEQUATE COMPETENCIES TO WORK IN THE FIELDS OF EDUCATION, RESEARCH, AND THE DEVELOPMENT AND DISSEMINATION OF CULTURAL PRODUCTS. IN PARTICULAR, GRADUATES IN MODERN PHILOLOGY MUST HAVE DEVELOPED A SOLID THEORETICAL FOUNDATION REGARDING THE VARIOUS MECHANISMS OF LITERARY PRODUCTION AND COMMUNICATION DATING BACK TO DIFFERENT HISTORICAL PERIODS (FROM THE MEDIEVAL TO THE CONTEMPORARY), EVEN THROUGH THE MOST MODERN CHANNELS OF TRANSMISSION. GRADUATES IN MODERN PHILOLOGY POSSESS ADEQUATE KNOWLEDGE OF THE MAIN THEORIES OF LANGUAGE, ALONG WITH GOOD ABILITIES TO COMMUNICATE SPECIFIC DISCIPLINARY CONTENT IN WRITTEN AND ORAL FORM, EVEN IN A EUROPEAN FOREIGN LANGUAGE, THANKS TO THE ACQUISITION OF SPECIALIST VOCABULARY.
THIS RESULTS IN A PROFESSIONAL PROFILE THAT CORRESPONDS, IN ADDITION TO THE FIGURE OF AN EXPERT IN HUMANITIES EDUCATION, ALSO TO THAT OF A HIGH-LEVEL CULTURAL OPERATOR WITH HIGH ENTREPRENEURIAL CAPABILITIES IN THE FIELD OF MANAGEMENT, ORGANIZATION, AND MULTIMEDIA COMMUNICATION OF CULTURAL EVENTS.
Dublin descriptors: I - Knowledge and understanding: GRADUATES IN MODERN PHILOLOGY MUST BE ABLE TO MOVE WITH ABSOLUTE MASTERY AND PERSPICACITY WITHIN THE HORIZONS OF HUMANISTIC KNOWLEDGE, AND THEREFORE MUST POSSESS A SET OF ADVANCED-LEVEL KNOWLEDGE IN THE RESPECTIVE AREAS RELATED TO THE STUDIES OF PHILOLOGY, LITERATURE, AND HISTORY, TO DISCIPLINARY PEDAGOGY, AND TO THE RELATED MAJOR CRITICAL METHODOLOGIES, WHICH ALSO ALLOWS THEM TO UNDERTAKE SPECIFIC RESEARCH PATHS. GRADUATES OF THE COURSE MUST ALSO POSSESS A SOLID THEORETICAL BASIS ON THE PROCESSES OF COMMUNICATION IN GENERAL AND ON THE MECHANISMS OF PRODUCTION, COMMUNICATION, AND CONSUMPTION OF DIFFERENT GENRES AND FORMATS, INCLUDING THOSE MOST WIDESPREAD THROUGH NEW TECHNOLOGIES. IT IS ALSO EXPECTED THAT GRADUATES HAVE ACQUIRED MARKED ABILITIES TO UNDERSTAND AND FRAME THE HISTORICAL AND CULTURAL ISSUES RELATED TO THEIR FIELDS OF STUDY, AND THAT THEY ARE THEREFORE ABLE TO ELABORATE AND/OR APPLY ORIGINAL APPROACHES AND INSIGHTS, IN ORDER TO CONTRIBUTE TO ADVANCING THE STATE OF RESEARCH IN THE RELEVANT SECTORS.
TO ACHIEVE THESE KNOWLEDGE OBJECTIVES, THE COURSE PROVIDES FOR INSTITUTIONAL COURSES WITH A HIGH DEGREE OF SPECIALIZATION, AND THEREFORE WITH A TARGETED FOCUS ON THE METHODOLOGICAL AND CRITICAL-BIBLIOGRAPHICAL ASPECTS OF THE VARIOUS DISCIPLINES. ANOTHER FUNDAMENTAL ASPECT OF THIS PROCESS WILL BE ALL THE WORK OF IN-DEPTH PREPARATION FOR THE FINAL THESIS.
II - Ability to apply knowledge and understanding: GRADUATES MUST HAVE DEVELOPED THE FOLLOWING ABILITIES:
- ABILITY TO ACT PROFESSIONALLY IN THE DIFFERENT AREAS OF PHILOLOGICAL, LITERARY, AND HISTORICAL STUDIES;
- ABILITY TO PRODUCE AND UNDERSTAND TEXTS OF DIFFERENT NATURES AND TYPES, APPLYING THE PRINCIPLES OF LINGUISTIC INVESTIGATION TO THE MOST DIVERSE COMMUNICATION CONTEXTS, EVEN IN A FOREIGN LANGUAGE;
- ABILITY TO CARRY OUT DATA MANAGEMENT ACTIVITIES, EVEN IN ELECTRONIC FORMAT, ANALYSIS, AND CRITICAL EVALUATION OF THE ISSUES ADDRESSED (STUDY OF NATIONAL AND SUPRANATIONAL HISTORICAL SCENARIOS, PERIODIZATION AND FRAMING OF HISTORICAL-LITERARY TRENDS, DESCRIPTION OF LINGUISTIC STRUCTURES, INTERPRETATION OF SEMANTIC-STYLISTIC OR ENTIRE AUTHORIAL CORPORA OR INDIVIDUAL WORKS, ETC.);
- ABILITY TO IDENTIFY AND SOLVE PROBLEMS, IN PARTICULAR IN RELATION TO THE NEED TO "UPDATE" THE STUDY OF HISTORICAL-CULTURAL HERITAGE AND VICE VERSA, THAT IS, TO BE ABLE TO FIND IN THE TRADITION EVEN THE HIDDEN TRACES OF CURRENT SOCIO-CULTURAL DYNAMICS;
- ABILITY TO USE THE MAIN COMPUTER TOOLS IN THE SPECIFIC AREAS OF COMPETENCE;
- ABILITY TO PARTICIPATE IN OPERATIONAL PROJECTS USING THE ACQUIRED KNOWLEDGE, EVEN FROM A MULTIDISCIPLINARY AND INTERDISCIPLINARY PERSPECTIVE;
- ABILITY TO COMMUNICATE AND ARGUE IN DIFFERENT CONTEXTS, EVEN IN A FOREIGN LANGUAGE;
THE TEACHING TOOLS PROVIDED FOR ACHIEVING THESE ABILITIES ARE: HIGHLY SPECIALIZED, MANDATORY INSTITUTIONAL COURSES AIMED AT DEVELOPING STUDENTS' PROBLEM-SOLVING LEARNING ABILITIES; SEMINAR ACTIVITIES; METHODOLOGICAL AND BIBLIOGRAPHICAL LABORATORIES; INTERNSHIPS AT SECONDARY EDUCATION INSTITUTIONS. THE CORRECT APPLICATION OF KNOWLEDGE WILL BE VERIFIED DURING AND AT THE END OF THE LABORATORY ACTIVITIES AND THE INSTITUTIONAL COURSES.
Level of satisfaction of graduates and employment status of graduates: GRADUATE PROFILE AND RELEVANT EMPLOYMENT STATUS. III - Autonomy of judgment: AT THE END OF THE COURSE, GRADUATES WILL HAVE TO DEVELOP THE ABILITY TO ADDRESS HISTORICAL-INTERPRETATIVE ISSUES, EVEN OF AN INTERDISCIPLINARY NATURE, IN AN AUTONOMOUS WAY, DEMONSTRATING THAT IS, IN RELATION TO THE PREVIOUS "LITERATURE", A NON-PASSIVE, BUT RATHER ACTIVE AND POSSIBLY INNOVATIVE ATTITUDE, THANKS TO THE ACQUISITION OF NEW INFORMATION, OR THE ELABORATION OF NEW PERSPECTIVES OF ANALYSIS AND RESEARCH.
MOREOVER, THE GRADUATE MUST POSSESS AUTONOMY OF JUDGMENT EVEN IN THE PRESENCE OF INCOMPLETE INFORMATION, AND MUST FINALLY DEMONSTRATE THE ABILITY TO REFLECT ON THEIR OWN WORK AND ON THE RELATED METHODOLOGIES, AS WELL AS ON THE "PUBLIC" IMPLICATIONS OF THEIR ANALYSIS AND JUDGMENT TOOLS.
THE MODALITIES FOR ACHIEVING THESE OBJECTIVES ARE ABOVE ALL RELATED TO THE PROBLEM-ORIENTED APPROACH OF THE INSTITUTIONAL COURSES, DURING WHICH STUDENTS WILL BE CONTINUOUSLY CONFRONTED WITH ALTERNATIVE INTERPRETATIONS, BOTH PHILOLOGICAL AND HISTORICAL, WHICH WILL INEVITABLY CONTRIBUTE TO FORMING A CRITICAL ATTITUDE CAPABLE OF PRODUCING AUTONOMY OF JUDGMENT. OTHER TEACHING TOOLS ARE ALSO PROVIDED FOR THIS FUNDAMENTAL POINT - BECAUSE IT INVOLVES THE ENTIRE CULTURAL FORMATION OF THE STUDENT, WHILE FULLY RESPECTING THEIR HUMAN PERSONALITY - AND WILL CONSIST OF BOTH TARGETED SEMINAR ACTIVITIES AND CONSTANT CONTACT AND DIALOGUE BETWEEN STUDENTS AND TEACHERS. AUTONOMY OF JUDGMENT WILL ALSO BE EXPERIENCED THROUGH THE FINAL EXAM, IN WHICH THE GRADUATE WILL BE ABLE TO APPROPRIATELY EXPRESS THE ACQUIRED MASTERY OF THEIR OWN EVALUATION SYSTEM AND THE ABILITY TO REFLECT AND ELABORATE WITHIN SPECIFIC CULTURAL DOMAINS.
IV - Communication skills: GRADUATES MUST HAVE LEARNED THE METHODS AND TECHNIQUES OF COMMUNICATION IN SUCH A WAY AS TO BE ABLE TO EXPOSE AND TRANSMIT THEIR KNOWLEDGE, WITH PROPRIETY OF LANGUAGE, AND NOT ONLY IN A CLEAR AND COMPREHENSIBLE WAY, BUT ALSO ARTICULATED ON DIFFERENT LEVELS OF LISTENING/INTERACTION.
THESE ABILITIES WILL BE VERIFIED BOTH DURING THE COURSE BY THE TEACHER-TUTORS AND THROUGH THE FINAL EXAM, IN WHICH THE ABILITY TO ENUNCIATE AND COMMUNICATE, AS WELL AS TO PRODUCE A WRITTEN TEXT ON TOPICS RELATED TO THE SPECIFIC DISCIPLINARY AREAS OF THE COURSE, CAN BE MEASURED.
V - Learning ability: THANKS TO THE METHODOLOGICAL COURSES AND A CONTINUOUS WORK OF PROFESSIONAL SELF-REFLECTION, GRADUATES IN MODERN PHILOLOGY WILL HAVE DEVELOPED ALL THOSE LEARNING ABILITIES THAT WILL ALLOW THEM TO CONTINUE TO STUDY AUTONOMOUSLY AND UNPREJUDICEDLY, BOTH IN SECOND-LEVEL MASTER'S DEGREES, IN RESEARCH DOCTORATES, AND, EVENTUALLY, IN OTHER AND DIFFERENT WORK ACTIVITIES.
THE COURSE PROVIDES FOR SPECIFIC SUPPORT FOR THE ACQUISITION OF THESE ABILITIES, FOR EXAMPLE, THROUGH THE CONTINUOUS EFFORT DERIVED FROM THE CRITICAL AND PROBLEM-ORIENTED READING OF THE TEXTS, AND MORE GENERAL FORMS, RELATED TO THE PROCESS OF STUDENT MATURATION CARRIED OUT THROUGH THE ATTENDANCE OF THE INDIVIDUAL COURSES, TRAINING IN INTERNSHIPS, AND PERIODIC SEMINAR MEETINGS WITH TEACHERS. THE ACQUISITION AND VERIFICATION OF THESE ABILITIES WILL TAKE PLACE THROUGH THE NORMAL AND MULTIPLE OCCASIONS FOR CHECKING THE DEGREE OF STUDENT MATURATION, FROM THE ATTENDANCE OF THE INDIVIDUAL COURSES TO THE TRAINING IN INTERNSHIPS, FROM THE SEMINAR MEETINGS BETWEEN TEACHERS AND STUDENTS TO THE FORMS OF TUTORING DURING THE COURSE, UP TO THE FINAL PHASE OF PREPARATION FOR THE FINAL EXAM.

Master's Degree Roles Responsible for approving study plans ALFONSO SIANO, FRANCESCA CONTE, ALESSANDRO MAISTO, JACQUELINE AIELLO Student mobility responsibility DANIELA VELLUTINO President of the didactic council ALFONSO SIANO Characteristics and Other Information Description of the study program STUDY PLAN Access to further studies.
ACCESS TO 3RD CYCLE STUDIES (PHD, SECOND-LEVEL UNIVERSITY MASTER)
Professional status conferred by the degree.
THIS DEGREE COURSE PREPARES FOR THE PROFESSION OF (ISTAT CODES)
1. SPECIALISTS IN MANAGEMENT AND CONTROL IN PRIVATE COMPANIES - (2.5.1.2.0)
INTERNAL AUDITOR, BUDGET AND CONTROL MANAGERS, COMMERCIAL INSPECTORS, INSPECTORS AND MANAGEMENT CONTROLLERS, PROJECT MANAGERS IN PRIVATE COMPANIES IN ANY SECTOR OR ECONOMIC FIELD, CONDUCT RESEARCH, STUDIES AND ASSESSMENTS IN THE FIELD OF ORGANIZATION, DESIGN AND DEFINITION OF ADMINISTRATIVE PROCEDURES AND SERVICES AND QUALITY ASSURANCE SYSTEMS NECESSARY FOR THE MANAGEMENT OF BUSINESS ACTIVITIES, OR APPLY EXISTING KNOWLEDGE AND PROCEDURES TO PROVIDE, IMPLEMENT AND IMPROVE THEM.
2. SPECIALISTS IN THE COMMERCIALIZATION OF INFORMATION AND COMMUNICATION TECHNOLOGIES - (2.5.1.5.3)
CONSULTANTS FOR THE SALE OF INFORMATION TECHNOLOGY, EXPERTS IN E-COMMERCE OR E-PROCUREMENT, COMMERCIAL MANAGERS IN THE ICT SECTOR, MARKETING SPECIALISTS IN IT SERVICES, DEAL WITH THE IMPLEMENTATION OF SALES STRATEGIES BY COMPANIES OF GOODS AND SERVICES IN THE INFORMATION AND COMMUNICATION TECHNOLOGY SECTOR, THE EFFICIENCY OF THE DISTRIBUTION AND COMMERCIAL NETWORK, THE MONITORING OF SALES AND MARKET ACCEPTANCE OF THE GOODS AND SERVICES MARKETED.
3. SPECIALISTS IN PUBLIC RELATIONS, IMAGE AND RELATED PROFESSIONS - (2.5.1.6.0)
EXPERTS IN PUBLIC RELATIONS AND CORPORATE COMMUNICATION, MANAGERS OR EMPLOYEES IN PRESS OFFICES, IMAGE CONSULTANTS, SPOKESPERSONS IN PUBLIC ADMINISTRATIONS, PROMOTE PUBLIC RELATIONS AND THE IMAGE OF A COMPANY OR PUBLIC ORGANIZATION BY WRITING TEXTS, SELECTING AND DISTRIBUTING FAVORABLE PUBLICITY MATERIAL, ORGANIZING AND SPONSORING EVENTS OF PARTICULAR IMPORTANCE AND VISIBILITY, CHARITABLE ACTIVITIES IN FAVOR OF THE POPULATION AND SIMILAR ACTIVITIES NOT DIRECTLY RELATED TO ADVERTISING PROMOTION.
4. WRITERS OF TEXTS FOR ADVERTISING - (2.5.4.1.3)
COPYWRITERS, STORYTELLERS, EMPLOYEES IN PUBLIC OR PRIVATE COMPANIES IN ANY SECTOR OR ECONOMIC FIELD, SPECIALISTS IN THE WRITING OF INFORMATIVE AND ADVERTISING TEXTS OF GOODS AND SERVICES TO BE DISTRIBUTED THROUGH PRINT, RADIO, TELEVISION AND OTHER MEDIA.
Official language(s) of instruction and assessment.
ITALIAN Specific learning objectives.
THE MASTER'S DEGREE COURSE IN CORPORATE COMMUNICATION AND MEDIA ENABLES ITS GRADUATES TO:
A) DEVELOP SCIENTIFIC COMPETENCIES SUITABLE FOR THE USE AND CONCRETE APPLICATION OF COMMUNICATION THEORIES, BOTH IN THE PUBLIC AND PRIVATE SECTORS AND IN THE COMPANY, ALSO IN ORDER TO CARRY OUT INFORMATION CAMPAIGNS AT DIFFERENT LEVELS;
B) DEVELOP METHODOLOGICAL SKILLS SUITABLE FOR THE APPLICATION OF COMMUNICATION TOOLS;
C) EXPLORE THE INFRASTRUCTURAL AND OPERATIONAL COMPONENTS, AS WELL AS THE SOCIAL AND CULTURAL IMPACT, OF INFORMATION AND COMMUNICATION TECHNOLOGY, NEW MEDIA AND COMPUTER AND NETWORKING ENGINEERING;
D) DESIGN STUDIES AND RESEARCH ACTIVITIES RELATED TO COMMUNICATION THEORIES AND NEW TECHNOLOGIES USED IN THE CONTEXT OF THE NATIONAL AND INTERNATIONAL INFORMATION SYSTEM, WITH PARTICULAR REFERENCE TO MULTIMEDIA AND DIGITAL PRODUCTIONS;
E) MASTER TECHNOLOGICAL KNOWLEDGE AND COMMUNICATION SKILLS;
G) EVALUATE INFORMATION PROCESSES STARTING FROM THE ANALYSIS OF SPECIFIC LINGUISTIC CODES;
H) APPLY BASIC COMPUTER KNOWLEDGE FOR THE PREPARATION OF REPORTS AND DATA ANALYSIS;
I) USE FLUENTLY, IN WRITTEN AND ORAL FORM, AT LEAST ONE LANGUAGE OF THE EUROPEAN UNION IN ADDITION TO ITALIAN (IN PARTICULAR, ENGLISH), WITH REFERENCE ALSO TO DISCIPLINARY VOCABULARIES.
THE ACHIEVEMENT OF THE ABILITY TO APPLY THE ABOVE-MENTIONED KNOWLEDGE AND UNDERSTANDING IS ACHIEVED THROUGH CRITICAL REFLECTION ON THE TEXTS PROPOSED FOR INDIVIDUAL STUDY, STIMULATED BY THE ACTIVITIES IN THE CLASSROOM, THE STUDY OF RESEARCH AND APPLICATION CASES SHOWN BY THE TEACHERS, THE PERFORMANCE OF PRACTICAL LABORATORY AND COMPUTER EXERCISES, BIBLIOGRAPHIC AND FIELD RESEARCH, AS WELL AS IN THE PREPARATION OF THE FINAL EXAM.
Level of satisfaction of the graduates and employment status of the graduates GRADUATE PROFILE AND EMPLOYMENT STATUS

-----

UNIVERSITY: University of Salerno
COURSE: [03730] SUPERVISION FOR SOCIAL SERVICES (SUPISS)
SECTION: General description
URL: https://unisa.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/500820

FIRST-LEVEL MASTER'S DEGREE
INFO General information for the current academic year Duration: 1 year. Admission year: 2024. Status: Open. Language: Italian. Type: Master's Degree. Regulation: D.M. 270/2004. Inter-university course: No. Admission requirements: University of Salerno - Fisciano. Department: DEPARTMENT OF POLITICAL AND SOCIAL STUDIES. Verification of knowledge required for admission: No. Course lecturers: View list. Admission requirements: Mandatory qualifications: [L2]. First-level degree. Course coordinator: GENNARO IORIO. Characteristics and other information: Specific training objectives.

THE FIRST-LEVEL UNIVERSITY MASTER'S DEGREE PROGRAM ENTITLED "SUPERVISION FOR SOCIAL SERVICES (SUPISS)"
AIMS TO TRAIN SUPERVISORS IN THE FIELD OF SOCIAL AND SOCIO-HEALTH SERVICES, COMPETENT IN PROMOTING THE HIGH QUALITY OF SERVICES, OFFERING ADEQUATE RESPONSES TO CITIZENS' NEEDS, AND SUPPORTING THE WELL-BEING OF PROFESSIONALS. TO THIS END, THE COURSE WILL AIM TO IDENTIFY SUPERVISION PATHS DESIGNED TO CONSOLIDATE EXISTING SKILLS AND ACQUIRE NEW ONES; ENHANCE THE SKILLS, ABILITIES, ATTITUDES, AND PERSONAL CHARACTERISTICS OF SOCIAL WORKERS AND SOCIAL WORKERS IN THE FIELD; FOSTER GREATER AWARENESS OF THE RISKS AND OPPORTUNITIES AND THE WAYS TO ADDRESS THEM IN PROFESSIONAL WORK;
DEVELOP GREATER CAPACITY FOR SOCIAL WORKERS TO ACT THROUGH COORDINATED AND INTEGRATED ACTIONS WITH OTHER ACTORS IN THE ORGANIZATIONAL AND SOCIAL CONTEXT IN WHICH THEY OPERATE;
PROVIDE TOOLS AND TECHNOLOGIES TO IMPLEMENT THESE ACTION COURSES; INCREASE THEIR CAPACITY FOR REFLECTION IN ORDER TO CORRECT OR IDENTIFY MORE APPROPRIATE AND FEASIBLE PATHS WITH RESPECT TO THE NEEDS OF THE USERS.

-----

COURSE: [05225] COMPUTER SCIENCE
TYPE: Master's Degree Course
SECTION: General Description
URL: https://unisa.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/500260

[05225] COMPUTER SCIENCE MASTER'S DEGREE COURSE
COURSE STRUCTURE AND COURSES Information regarding the current academic year Duration: 2 years. Admission year: 2016. Status: Open. Credits: 120 CFU. Language: Italian. Type: Master's Degree. Regulations: D.M. 270/2004. Degree class: LM-18 - Master's Degree in Computer Science. Inter-university course: No. Open access: Yes. Location: UNIVERSITY OF SALERNO - FISCIANO. Department: DEPARTMENT OF COMPUTER SCIENCE. Verification of knowledge required for access: No. Course lecturers: View list. Admission requirements: Optional qualifications (choose from the following): [TSS+L2], First-level degree, Upper secondary school diploma [FOREIGN DEGREE], Foreign degree, [TSS+DIPLOMA], Upper secondary school diploma, University diploma, [TSS+L1], Upper secondary school diploma, Degree. Roles: Study plan approval: BARBARA MASUCCI, GIULIANA VITIELLO, FILOMENA FERRUCCI, MAURIZIO TUCCI, VIRGINIA GIORNO, DELFINA MALANDRINO, FABIO PALOMBA. Student mobility: FILOMENA FERRUCCI. President of the didactic council: GIULIANA VITIELLO. Characteristics and other information: Description of the course: STUDY PLAN. Access to further studies: ACCESS TO 3RD CYCLE STUDIES (PHD, 2ND LEVEL UNIVERSITY MASTER). Professional status conferred by the degree: GRADUATES CAN BE EMPLOYED AS ANALYSTS, DESIGNERS, AND SOFTWARE DEVELOPERS; DESIGNERS AND MANAGERS OF DATABASES, NETWORKS, AND TELECOMMUNICATION SYSTEMS, SOFTWARE PROJECT MANAGERS. A DEGREE IN COMPUTER SCIENCE PROVIDES ACCESS TO STATE EXAMINATIONS TO OBTAIN QUALIFICATION TO PRACTICE THE PROFESSION OF INFORMATION ENGINEER. Official language(s) of instruction and assessment: ITALIAN. Specific learning objectives: THE OBJECTIVE OF THE MASTER'S DEGREE IN COMPUTER SCIENCE IS TO TRAIN A PROFESSIONAL CAPABLE OF PLANNING, DESIGNING, DEVELOPING, DIRECTING WORKS, ESTIMATING, TESTING, AND MANAGING COMPLEX OR INNOVATIVE PLANTS AND SYSTEMS FOR THE GENERATION, TRANSMISSION, AND PROCESSING OF INFORMATION, EVEN WHEN IT INVOLVES THE USE OF ADVANCED, INNOVATIVE, OR EXPERIMENTAL METHODOLOGIES. THEORETICAL, METHODOLOGICAL, SYSTEMIC, AND TECHNOLOGICAL KNOWLEDGE IS EXPANDED AND DEEPENED IN ALL DISCIPLINES THAT CONSTITUTE THE FUNDAMENTAL CULTURAL ELEMENTS OF COMPUTER SCIENCE. THIS ENABLES THE MASTER'S DEGREE GRADUATE TO IDENTIFY NEW THEORETICAL DEVELOPMENTS IN COMPUTER SCIENCE DISCIPLINES AND THEIR RELATED APPLICATION FIELDS, AS WELL AS TO OPERATE AT A DESIGN AND DECISION-MAKING LEVEL IN ALL AREAS OF COMPUTER SCIENCE. IN PARTICULAR, COMPUTER SCIENCE MASTER'S DEGREE GRADUATES MUST: - POSSESS SOLID KNOWLEDGE OF BOTH THE FOUNDATIONS AND THE APPLICATION ASPECTS OF THE VARIOUS SECTORS OF COMPUTER SCIENCE; - HAVE A THOROUGH UNDERSTANDING OF THE PRINCIPLES, STRUCTURES, AND USE OF PROCESSING SYSTEMS; - KNOW THE FUNDAMENTALS, TECHNIQUES, AND METHODS OF DESIGN AND IMPLEMENTATION OF COMPUTER SYSTEMS, BOTH BASIC AND APPLICATION-ORIENTED; - HAVE KNOWLEDGE OF DIFFERENT APPLICATION SECTORS; - BE ABLE TO WORK WITH A HIGH DEGREE OF AUTONOMY, WITH GOOD DIRECTIVE, COMMUNICATION, AND MANAGERIAL SKILLS IN LEADING WORK GROUPS. THESE SKILLS ARE ACQUIRED THROUGH LECTURES AND LABORATORY EXERCISES, INDEPENDENT AND GROUP PROJECT ACTIVITIES, AND THESIS WORK. Level of satisfaction of graduates and employment status of graduates: GRADUATE PROFILE AND RELEVANT EMPLOYMENT STATUS.

THE FIRST-LEVEL UNIVERSITY MASTER'S DEGREE COURSE, CALLED CINETOURISM 3.0 & DESTINATION MANAGEMENT. INTELLIGENT TECHNOLOGIES AND VIRTUAL REALITY, AIMS TO REVITALIZE TRAINING IN AN IMPORTANT MARKET NICHE, CONSIDERING THE CHANGED INSTITUTIONAL, PRODUCTIVE, AND MARKET CONTEXT, REDEFINED ABOVE ALL BY NEW DIGITAL TECHNOLOGIES AND AUDIOVISUAL MEDIA. IT IS AIMED AT THOSE WHO INTEND TO WORK IN PUBLIC OR PRIVATE TOURISM ORGANIZATIONS AND COMPANIES AND SPECIALIZE IN THE NEW PROFESSION OF DESTINATION MANAGER. THIS IS A NEW SPECIALIST PROFILE, NOT AVAILABLE ON THE LABOR MARKET, BUT HIGHLY DEMANDED, BECAUSE IT IS ABLE TO USE MODERN ICT TECHNOLOGIES AND NEW WEB MARKETING TOOLS, THROUGH AN INTERDISCIPLINARY TRAINING PATH FOR THE DESIGN AND MANAGEMENT OF CINEMATIC TOURISM DESTINATIONS, WITH PARTICULAR ATTENTION TO WEB 3.0 AND THE USE OF SOCIAL NETWORKS.

[06707] EXPERT TECHNICIAN IN ADVANCED SERVICES FOR CONSTRUCTION COMPANIES, SECOND-LEVEL MASTER'S DEGREE
INFO General information for the current academic year. Duration: 1 year. Degree program: 2020. Status: Open. Language: Italian. Type: Master's Degree. Regulation: D.M. 270/2004. Inter-university course: No. Open access: Yes. Location: UNIVERSITY OF SALERNO - FISCIANO. Department: DEPARTMENT OF CIVIL ENGINEERING. Verification of knowledge required for access: No. Course lecturers: View list. Admission requirements: Mandatory qualifications: [TSS] Upper Secondary School Diploma. Optional qualifications (choose from the following): [LM+TSS] Master's Degree, [LS+TSS] Specialist Degree, [L1+TSS] Bachelor's Degree. Course contact: GIANLUIGI DE MARE. Curriculum approval manager: GIANLUIGI DE MARE. School director: GIANLUIGI DE MARE. Course director: GIANLUIGI DE MARE. Characteristics and other information: Professional status conferred by the degree:
 COMPANIES OPERATING IN THE CIVIL ENGINEERING AND CONSTRUCTION SECTOR, BOTH DIRECTLY AND THROUGH CONSORTIA AND ASSOCIATIONS;
 DESIGN STUDIOS SPECIALIZED IN SPECIFIC THEMES SUCH AS ENERGY EFFICIENCY AND SEISMIC UPGRADING OF BUILDINGS, REMOTE MONITORING AND CONTROL FOR HYDROGEOLOGICAL HAZARDS AND THE MANAGEMENT OF THE INTEGRATED WATER SYSTEM, MAINTENANCE OF INFRASTRUCTURE WORKS AND STATIC DESIGN OF RADIO TOWERS AND LATTICE STRUCTURES;
 NATIONAL AND TERRITORIAL PUBLIC BODIES AND ENTITIES INTERESTED IN THE PROTECTION OF THE TERRITORY AND INFRASTRUCTURE.
Specific learning objectives:
THE MASTER'S PROGRAM AIMS TO ACHIEVE THE FOLLOWING LEARNING OBJECTIVES:
 TO EXPAND ON THE ELEMENTS OF DESIGN QUALITY AS DEFINED BY THE RELEVANT REGULATIONS (DLGS 50/16; DM 560/2017; NTC2018; DLGS 34/20; DM 329/20);
 TO DEVELOP THE HARD AND SOFT SKILLS REQUIRED BY THE MARKET IN LINE WITH THE RECOMMENDATION OF THE EU COUNCIL OF 22.05.18;
 TO QUALIFY ENGINEERS IN THE COMPETENCIES REQUIRED BY THE MARKET, SPECIFICALLY:
O ENERGY EFFICIENCY AND SEISMIC UPGRADING OF BUILDINGS;
O MANAGEMENT AND SAFETY OF CONSTRUCTION SITES;
O ANALYSIS OF TENDERS, TECHNICAL AND ECONOMIC EVALUATION OF OFFERS AND INVESTMENTS;
O REMOTE MONITORING AND CONTROL FOR HYDROGEOLOGICAL HAZARDS AND TOOLS FOR THE MANAGEMENT OF THE INTEGRATED WATER SYSTEM;
O MAINTENANCE OF INFRASTRUCTURE WORKS;
O STATIC DESIGN OF RADIO TOWERS AND LATTICE STRUCTURES;
O RELATIONS WITH LOCAL AUTHORITIES IN THE GOVERNANCE OF THE TERRITORY.

UNIVERSITY: University of Salerno
COURSE: [07710] MICROBIOLOGICAL CONTROLS: QUALITY AND SAFETY IN PRODUCTION PROCESSES, SECOND-LEVEL MASTER'S DEGREE
SECTION: General description
URL: https://unisa.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/500617

[07710] MICROBIOLOGICAL CONTROLS: QUALITY AND SAFETY IN PRODUCTION PROCESSES, SECOND-LEVEL MASTER'S DEGREE
INFO General information for the current academic year. Duration: 1 year. Degree program: 2020. Status: Open. Language: Italian. Type: Master's Degree. Regulation: D.M. 270/2004. Inter-university course: No. Open access: Yes. Location: UNIVERSITY OF SALERNO - FISCIANO. Department: DEPARTMENT OF PHARMACY. Verification of knowledge required for access: No. Course lecturers: View list. Admission requirements: Mandatory qualifications: [TSS] Upper Secondary School Diploma. Optional qualifications (choose from the following): [LM+TSS] Master's Degree, [LS+TSS] Specialist Degree, [L1+TSS] Bachelor's Degree. Course roles: Course Coordinator: AMALIA PORTA, School Director: AMALIA PORTA, Head of the Teaching Council: AMALIA PORTA, Course Director: AMALIA PORTA. Characteristics and other information: Professional status conferred by the degree.
THE MASTER'S IN "MICROBIOLOGICAL CONTROLS: QUALITY AND SAFETY IN PRODUCTION PROCESSES" AIMS TO TRAIN EXPERTS IN THE MANAGEMENT OF SAFETY AND QUALITY CONTROL PROCESSES IN VARIOUS OCCUPATIONAL FIELDS, SUCH AS:
- FOOD INDUSTRIES,
- PHARMACEUTICAL INDUSTRIES;
- COSMETIC INDUSTRIES;
- PUBLIC AND PRIVATE LABORATORIES OF APPLIED BIOTECHNOLOGY.
Specific learning objectives:
THE AIM IS TO PROVIDE THEORETICAL/PRACTICAL TRAINING THAT ACCOMPANIES PARTICIPANTS TOWARDS THE ACQUISITION OF A BROAD AND INNOVATIVE PROFESSIONAL SKILL IN MICROBIOLOGICAL QUALITY CONTROL, THROUGH THE ABILITY TO ADOPT AND EMPLOY UP-TO-DATE AND VALID MICROBIOLOGICAL ANALYSIS METHODS AND TECHNIQUES, AS PROVIDED FOR BY NATIONAL AND EUROPEAN REGULATIONS, WITH PARTICULAR REFERENCE TO THE GUIDELINES OF THE "EUROPEAN PHARMACOPOEIA" AND THE "CLINICAL AND LABORATORY STANDARDS INSTITUTE (C.L.S.I.)".

UNIVERSITY: University of Salerno
COURSE: [12125] DIPLOMATIC, INTERNATIONAL STUDIES, AND GLOBAL SECURITY
TYPE: Bachelor's Degree
SECTION: General Description
URL: https://unisa.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/500544

[12125] DIPLOMATIC, INTERNATIONAL STUDIES, AND GLOBAL SECURITY BACHELOR'S DEGREE
COURSE INFORMATION AND COURSES General information relating to the current academic year Duration: 3 years. Admission year: 2019. Status: Open. Credits: 180 CFU. Language: Italian. Type: Three-year degree. Regulations: D.M. 270/2004. Degree class: L-36 - Class of degrees in Political Science and International Relations. Inter-university course: No. Admission test: Self-assessment test. Location: UNIVERSITY OF SALERNO - FISCIANO. Department: DEPARTMENT OF BUSINESS SCIENCES - MANAGEMENT & INNOVATION SYSTEMS. Verification of knowledge required for admission: No. Course lecturers: View list. Admission requirements: Optional qualifications (to be chosen from the following): [TSS]. Upper secondary school diploma: [TS]. Foreign qualification. Roles: Head of approval of study plans: FRANCESCO AMORETTI, CHIARA D'AURIA. Student mobility responsibility: PAOLA ATTOLINO. President of the didactic council: FRANCESCO AMORETTI. Characteristics and other information: Access to further studies: UNIVERSITY MASTER'S DEGREE, SECOND-LEVEL MASTER'S DEGREE. Professional status conferred by the degree: THE COURSE PREPARES FOR THE PROFESSION OF: GLOBAL SECURITY ANALYST • INTELLIGENCE ANALYST • INVESTIGATIVE EXPERT • CYBERSECURITY EXPERT • INFORMATION ACQUISITION TECHNICIAN • NGO OFFICER • VOLUNTEERING AND THIRD SECTOR OFFICER • DIPLOMAT • MILITARY. Official language(s) of instruction and assessment: ITALIAN. Specific learning objectives: THE DEGREE COURSE IN DIPLOMATIC, INTERNATIONAL STUDIES, AND GLOBAL SECURITY OFFERS A CUTTING-EDGE EDUCATIONAL PROGRAM, ARTICULATED IN MULTIDISCIPLINARY KNOWLEDGE ESSENTIAL TO ADDRESS THE CHALLENGES AND COMPLEXITY OF THE VARIOUS AREAS OF SECURITY POLICY IN THE 21ST CENTURY. THE FIRST THREE-YEAR DEGREE COURSE TO BE FOCUSED ON THE FIELD OF POLITICAL AND SOCIAL SCIENCES ON THE ISSUES OF "DEMOCRATIC SECURITY", IT PROVIDES STUDENTS WITH THEORETICAL PERSPECTIVES, ANALYSIS METHODOLOGIES, AND INTERVENTION TOOLS IN THE HISTORICAL-POLITICAL, LEGAL, SOCIAL, ECONOMIC, PHILOSOPHICAL, LINGUISTIC, AND MATHEMATICAL-COMPUTER SCIENCE FIELDS. AN UNPRECEDENTED AND INNOVATIVE MIX OF ACADEMIC KNOWLEDGE, AS DEMONSTRATED BY THE TWO CURRICULA THAT DEFINE THE EDUCATIONAL OFFER. THE CURRICULUM IN DIPLOMATIC STUDIES AND STRATEGIES FOR GLOBAL SECURITY PROVIDES THE BASIC KNOWLEDGE AND SKILLS IN THE FIELD OF CYBERSECURITY: FROM THE MATHEMATICAL TECHNIQUES FOR MODELING SYSTEMS TO THE ARCHITECTURAL PRINCIPLES AND SOFTWARE APPLICATION DESIGN TECHNIQUES, FROM THE CRYPTOGRAPHIC PRIMITIVES USED TO GUARANTEE THE SECURITY OF SYSTEMS TO THE SKILLS TO UNDERSTAND AND IMPLEMENT TECHNIQUES FOR PREVENTION AND MANAGEMENT OF SECURITY RISKS IN AN ORGANIZATIONAL AND ECONOMIC CONTEXT. THE CURRICULUM IN GEOPOLITICS FOR THE SECURITY OF THE MEDITERRANEAN PROVIDES THE ESSENTIAL KNOWLEDGE AND SKILLS IN THE FIELD OF SECURITY APPLIED TO SOFT POWER, WITH PARTICULAR REGARD TO THE GEOPOLITICAL AREA OF THE "ENLARGED MEDITERRANEAN". THE MAIN EDUCATIONAL OBJECTIVES CHARACTERIZING THIS CURRICULAR PATH INVOLVE THE UNDERSTANDING OF THE HISTORICAL, POLITICAL, ECONOMIC, AND SOCIOLOGICAL REASONS FOR THE COMPLEXITY AND TENSIONS OF THE GEOPOLITICAL AREA INCLUDING THE COUNTRIES OF THE MEDITERRANEAN, WITH A FOCUS BOTH ON THE NORMS - INTERNATIONAL TREATIES AND NATIONAL OR EUROPEAN LAWS - THAT REGULATE THE MIGRATORY PROCESS, AND ON THE POLICIES FOR THE MANAGEMENT OF RESOURCES AND THE STRATEGIES FOR THE RECOMPOSITION OF CONFLICTS.

[12225] GLOBAL STUDIES AND EU MASTER'S DEGREE COURSE
COURSE STRUCTURE AND SUBJECTS Information regarding the current academic year Duration: 2 years. Admission year: 2018. Status: Open. Credits: 120 ECTS. Language: Italian. Type: Master's Degree. Regulations: D.M. 270/2004. Degree class: LM-52 - Master's Degree in International Relations. Inter-university course: No. Admission: Self-assessment test. Location: UNIVERSITY OF SALERNO - FISCIANO. Department: DEPARTMENT OF BUSINESS SCIENCES - MANAGEMENT & INNOVATION SYSTEMS. Verification of knowledge required for admission: No. Course lecturers: View list. Admission requirements: Optional qualifications (choose from the following):
[ L2+TSS ]
First-level degree,
Upper secondary school diploma
[ L1+TSS ]
Degree,
Upper secondary school diploma
[ TS ]
Foreign qualification
[ LM+TSS ]
Upper secondary school diploma,
Master's degree. Roles: Study plan approval manager: FRANCESCO AMORETTI, CHIARA D'AURIA. Student mobility manager: PAOLA ATTOLINO. Chairman of the didactic council: FRANCESCO AMORETTI. Characteristics and other information: Access to further studies. ALLOWS ACCESS TO 3RD CYCLE STUDIES (PHD, 2ND LEVEL UNIVERSITY MASTER). Professional status conferred by the degree: THE COURSE AIMS TO PROVIDE ADVANCED MULTIDISCIPLINARY SKILLS, NECESSARY TO WORK IN THE PROFESSIONS THAT CONSTITUTE THE FIELD OF ACTION OF INTERNATIONAL POLITICS: FROM THE MORE TRADITIONAL CAREERS IN DIPLOMACY AND INTERNATIONAL CIVIL SERVICE, ADDRESSING THE NEEDS OF 21ST CENTURY DIPLOMACY, TO JOBS IN THE HIGHLY INTERNATIONAL SECTORS OF CONSULTING FIRMS, PRIVATE COMPANIES AND OTHER PUBLIC ADMINISTRATIONS, TO THE WORLD OF NGOS AND THE THIRD SECTOR, TO THE FIELD OF THINK TANKS AND NATIONAL AND INTERNATIONAL RESEARCH INSTITUTES.
Knowledge required for admission: TO BE ADMITTED TO THE MASTER'S DEGREE COURSE IN GLOBAL STUDIES AND EU, CANDIDATES MUST HOLD A THREE-YEAR DEGREE IN CLASS L-36 OR L16 (EX D.M. 270/04 OR IN THE CORRESPONDING CLASSES EX DM 509/99). GRADUATES IN OTHER CLASSES MAY BE ADMITTED TO THE MASTER'S DEGREE COURSE IF THEY HAVE OBTAINED AT LEAST 40 ECTS WITHIN AT LEAST TWO OF THE FOLLOWING DISCIPLINARY AREAS: ECONOMIC, LEGAL, LINGUISTIC, SOCIOLOGICAL, HISTORICAL, POLITICAL. TO BE ADMITTED TO THE MASTER'S DEGREE COURSE, STUDENTS MUST ALSO HAVE ADEQUATE PERSONAL PREPARATION AND A GOOD KNOWLEDGE OF THE ENGLISH LANGUAGE.
Specific learning objectives: THE COURSE OFFERS ECONOMIC-LEGAL, POLITICAL, SOCIOLOGICAL, COMPUTER SCIENCE AND LINGUISTIC SUBJECTS THAT PROVIDE STUDENTS WITH KNOWLEDGE AND SKILLS AIMED AT A HIGHLY PROFESSIONALIZED PROFILE. THANKS ALSO TO THE USE OF DIDACTIC METHODOLOGIES (SEMINAR WORK, WRITTEN REPORTS, ORAL PRESENTATIONS, IN-DEPTH STUDY OF PARTICULARLY SIGNIFICANT CASE STUDIES, SIMULATIONS) AND THE CONSULTATION OF THE MAIN EUROPEAN AND INTERNATIONAL DATABASES, STUDENTS ARE ABLE TO DEVELOP, ESPECIALLY IN THE PREPARATION OF THE DEGREE THESIS, THEIR OWN REFLECTION AND RESEARCH PATH.

[12226] TERRITORIAL POLICIES AND INTERNATIONAL COOPERATION MASTER'S DEGREE COURSE
COURSE INFORMATION AND COURSES General information relating to the current academic year Duration: 2 years. Program: 2019. Status: Open. Credits: 120 CFU. Language: Italian. Type: Master's Degree. Regulations: D.M. 270/2004. Degree Class: LM-62 - Master's Degree in Political Science. Inter-university Course: No. Admission: Self-assessment test. Location: UNIVERSITY OF SALERNO - FISCIANO. Department: DEPARTMENT OF POLITICAL AND COMMUNICATION SCIENCES. Verification of knowledge required for admission: No. Course lecturers: View list. Admission requirements: Optional qualifications (choose from the following): [ TSS+L2_C ] First-level degree, Upper secondary school diploma [ TSS+L1 ] Degree, Upper secondary school diploma [ TS ] Foreign qualification [ TSS+LM ] Upper secondary school diploma, Master's degree [ TSS+LM5 ] Upper secondary school diploma, Single-cycle Master's degree. Roles: Curriculum approval manager: GIANFRANCO MACRI', DOMENICO MADDALONI, CARLA PEDICINO, STEFANIA ECCHIA, MASSIMILIANO BENCARDINO, ROSANNA FATTIBENE, MARIAROSARIA COLUCCIELLO, SILVANA D'ALESSIO. Student mobility manager: MARIAROSARIA COLUCCIELLO. Chairman of the didactic council: MASSIMILIANO BENCARDINO. Characteristics and other information: Access to further studies. ACCESS TO 3RD CYCLE STUDIES (PHD, 2ND LEVEL UNIVERSITY MASTER). Professional status conferred by the degree: SPECIALISTS IN POLITICAL SCIENCE AND INSTITUTIONS; EXPERTS IN ECONOMIC DEVELOPMENT PLANS AND PROGRAMS; EXPERTS IN LOCAL DEVELOPMENT; EXPERTS IN POLICIES FOR THE ORGANIZATION OF NON-PROFIT COMPANIES AND INTERNATIONAL COOPERATION. Official language(s) of teaching and assessment: ITALIAN. Specific learning objectives: THE MASTER'S DEGREE COURSE IN TERRITORIAL POLICIES AND INTERNATIONAL COOPERATION AIMS TO PROVIDE STUDENTS WITH THE TOOLS TO APPLY BASIC KNOWLEDGE AND THEORETICAL MODELS TO SPECIFIC TERRITORIAL CONTEXTS, ACCORDING TO AN INTERDISCIPLINARY APPROACH CAPABLE OF PROMOTING ANALYSIS AND POLICY DEVELOPMENT CAPABILITIES IN RELATION TO REALITIES CHARACTERIZED BY A HIGH DEGREE OF COMPLEXITY. THEREFORE, THE EDUCATIONAL PROJECT PROVIDES FOR THE EQUAL PRESENCE OF THE FIVE DISCIPLINARY AREAS PROVIDED FOR BY THE DECREE ESTABLISHING THE LM62 (ECONOMIC, POLITICAL, SOCIOLOGICAL, LEGAL, HISTORICAL) AND CREATES THE CONDITIONS FOR THE ORGANIZATION OF STRONGLY CHARACTERIZED INTERDISCIPLINARY LABORATORIES (EURO-PROJECT DESIGN, LOCAL DEVELOPMENT, CULTURAL AND LINGUISTIC MEDIATION, ETC.) REGARDING JOB OPPORTUNITIES. THIS APPROACH IS BASED ON THE AWARENESS THAT, IN A LABOR MARKET IN CONTINUOUS EVOLUTION, WITHIN WHICH MORE AND MORE OFTEN ENTIRELY NEW PROFESSIONAL FIGURES ARE REQUIRED, MORE THAN MARKED SPECIALIZATION, THE TRAINING OF GRADUATES CAPABLE OF MASTERING THE FUNDAMENTAL KNOWLEDGE TOOLS OF THE DIFFERENT AREAS AND APPLYING THEM WITH HIGH FLEXIBILITY TO THE DIFFERENT NEEDS MAY PROVE SIGNIFICANT.

High school diploma

First-level degree Roles Course Coordinator GENNARO IORIO Chairman of the didactic council GENNARO IORIO Characteristics and other information Specific learning objectives.
THE ADVANCED TRAINING COURSE ENTITLED SOCIO-RELATIONAL COUNSELING FOR HELPING PROFESSIONS AIMS TO FILL THE TRAINING GAP FOR THOSE PROFESSIONAL PROFILES THAT DEAL, IN ONE WAY OR ANOTHER, WITH HELPING RELATIONSHIPS AND THAT, COMING FROM MOSTLY THEORETICAL ACADEMIC BACKGROUNDS, NEED TO ACQUIRE TECHNICAL COMPETENCIES, ABILITIES AND PERSONAL ATTITUDES TO MANAGE THE WORK OF "TAKING CARE" EFFECTIVELY.
THE COURSE AIMS AT THE FOLLOWING SPECIFIC LEARNING OBJECTIVES: TO INCREASE THE COMPETENCIES AND ABILITIES OF HELPING PROFESSIONALS TO ADDRESS PSYCHOSOCIAL NEEDS; TO BE ABLE TO VALUE EXPERIENCE AS A FUNDAMENTAL PREREQUISITE FOR LEARNING; TO STIMULATE THE RECOGNITION OF THE IMPACT OF CONTEXTUAL AND SITUATIONAL INFLUENCES ON SOCIAL IDENTITY; TO INCREASE THE INTEGRATION BETWEEN THEORY AND PRACTICE; TO PROMOTE AN "EMPOWERED-ORIENTED" APPROACH IN THE RELATIONSHIP WITH THE USER.

UNIVERSITY: University of Salerno
COURSE: [43221] MODERN LANGUAGES AND LITERATURES
TYPE: Master's Degree Course
SECTION: General Description
URL: https://unisa.coursecatalogue.cineca.it/corsi/2024/500210

[43221] MODERN LANGUAGES AND LITERATURES MASTER'S DEGREE COURSE
COURSE STRUCTURE AND SUBJECTS Information regarding the current academic year.
Duration: 2 years
Program: 2018
Status: Open
Credits: 120 CFU
Language: Italian
Type: Master's Degree
Regulation: D.M. 270/2004
Degree Class: LM-37 - Master's Degree in Modern European and American Languages and Literatures
Inter-university Course: No
Admission: Self-assessment test
Location: UNIVERSITY OF SALERNO - FISCIANO
Department: DEPARTMENT OF HUMANISTIC STUDIES
Teaching Method: In-person
Knowledge Verification for Admission: No
Course Lecturers: View list
Admission Requirements: Optional qualifications (choose from the following)
[L2+TSS]
Bachelor's Degree,
High School Diploma [L1+TSS]
Bachelor's Degree,
High School Diploma [TS]
Foreign Qualification
Roles: Study plan approval manager: STEFANO D'ATRI, PAOLA GHERI, ROSARIO PELLEGRINO, AGNESE SILVESTRI, CARMELA SAMMARCO, ALFONSINA BUONICONTO, GIUSEPPINA GIULIANO, MIKHAIL VELIZHEV
Student Mobility Manager: LINDA BARONE
Didactic Council President: ROSARIO PELLEGRINO, SABRINA GALANO
Characteristics and Other Information: Course description: STUDY PLAN Access to further studies.
THE MASTER'S DEGREE COURSE PROVIDES PREPARATION THAT ENABLES STUDENTS TO PURSUE FURTHER STUDIES IN LEVEL II MASTER'S PROGRAMS AND RESEARCH DOCTORATE PROGRAMS IN THE AREA OF THEORETICAL AND APPLIED LINGUISTICS, FOREIGN LANGUAGES AND LITERATURES, AND LANGUAGE TEACHING.
Professional status conferred by the degree:
- LITERARY AND SECTORAL LANGUAGE TRANSLATORS
- LANGUAGE AND CULTURAL CONSULTANTS
- LANGUAGE AND LITERARY TRAINERS
- LINGUISTIC-CULTURAL EXPERT FOR TERRITORIAL RESOURCES AND ECONOMIES
THE COURSE ALSO PREPARES STUDENTS FOR ADMISSION TO RESEARCH DOCTORATE PROGRAMS AND DOCTORAL SCHOOLS IN THE FIELD OF HUMANITIES.
Characteristics of the final exam:
THE DEGREE IS AWARDED UPON SUCCESSFUL COMPLETION OF A FINAL EXAM. THE FINAL EXAM CONSISTS OF WRITING A THESIS APPROPRIATE TO THE AMOUNT OF EDUCATIONAL CREDITS REQUIRED ON A SPECIFIC TOPIC, RELATED TO ONE OF THE SUBJECTS IN THE STUDY PLAN AND AGREED UPON WITH A SUPERVISOR.
THE PAPER MAY BE WRITTEN IN ITALIAN ACCOMPANIED BY A COMPREHENSIVE ABSTRACT IN A FOREIGN LANGUAGE, OR IN ONE OF THE FOREIGN LANGUAGES STUDIED, ACCOMPANIED BY A COMPREHENSIVE ABSTRACT IN ITALIAN.
Official language(s) of instruction and assessment:
ITALIAN. FOREIGN LANGUAGES AND THEIR RELATED LITERATURES ARE TAUGHT IN THE FOREIGN LANGUAGE (ENGLISH, FRENCH, SPANISH, RUSSIAN, AND GERMAN).
Specific learning objectives:
THE AIM OF THE COURSE IS TO DEVELOP ADVANCED COMPETENCIES IN THE FIELD OF LANGUAGES AND CULTURES OF EUROPEAN, AMERICAN, AND POSTCOLONIAL COUNTRIES. THE MAIN OBJECTIVES ARE:
- DEVELOP ADVANCED COMPETENCIES IN TWO MODERN LANGUAGES FROM THE FOLLOWING: FRENCH, ENGLISH, RUSSIAN, SPANISH, GERMAN (INCLUDING SPECIALIST VOCABULARY IN SOME DISCIPLINARY AND PROFESSIONAL FIELDS) AND THEIR RELATED LITERARY TRADITION, ALSO IN A COMPARATIVE DIMENSION;
- ACQUIRE ADVANCED METHODOLOGICAL TOOLS IN LINGUISTIC ANALYSIS AND IN THE TRANSLATION OF TEXTS OF VARIOUS TYPES IN AT LEAST TWO FOREIGN LANGUAGES;
- POSSESS ADVANCED KNOWLEDGE OF THE MAIN COMPUTER TOOLS IN THE SPECIFIC AREAS OF COMPETENCE;
- ACQUIRE GREATER AWARENESS OF THE CULTURAL, ECONOMIC, AND BUSINESS REALITIES OF THE TERRITORY;
- DEVELOP ADVANCED COMPETENCIES IN THE RESEARCH, DESIGN, AND WRITING OF EUROPEAN PROJECTS IN VARIOUS FIELDS AND SECTORS, BOTH ECONOMIC AND CULTURAL.
Dublin Descriptors: I - Knowledge and understanding: MASTER'S DEGREE GRADUATES MUST ACHIEVE:
- EXCELLENT KNOWLEDGE OF THE LANGUAGES STUDIED OVER THE TWO-YEAR PERIOD;
- ABILITY TO CONDUCT IN-DEPTH AND ORIGINAL ANALYSIS OF THE LANGUAGES STUDIED IN A COMPARATIVE PERSPECTIVE;
- ABILITY TO UNDERSTAND THE CULTURES AND LITERATURES RELATED TO THE LANGUAGES OF ADVANCED SPECIALIZATION;
- ABILITY TO UNDERSTAND AND PRODUCE ANY TYPE OF TEXT, EVEN AT A HIGH LEVEL OF COMPLEXITY;
- ABILITY TO ENGAGE IN CRITICAL REFLECTION AND ORIGINAL ELABORATION OF SOCIAL AND CULTURAL ISSUES AIMED AT BOTH RESEARCH AND INSERTION INTO NEW PROFESSIONAL CONTEXTS;
- ABILITY TO DESIGN INDEPENDENT SCIENTIFIC INVESTIGATION;
- SPECIFIC KNOWLEDGE OF THE MOST IMPORTANT DEVELOPMENTS IN THE CRITICAL-SCIENTIFIC DEBATE IN THE FIELDS OF LINGUISTICS, LITERATURE, AND CULTURE;
- ABILITY TO USE LANGUAGES AND COMPUTER TOOLS IN VARIOUS FIELDS OF APPLICATION;
- ABILITY TO MANAGE INTERNATIONAL CULTURAL AND ECONOMIC CONTACTS AND EXCHANGES.
DEPENDING ON THE AREA OF SPECIALIZATION CHOSEN, THEY WILL ALSO ACQUIRE:
- KNOWLEDGE OF THE METALINGUISTIC PROCESSES THAT ARE THE BASIS FOR LEARNING AND USING FOREIGN LANGUAGES;
- IN-DEPTH KNOWLEDGE OF THE METHODS AND THEORY OF LINGUISTICS AND/OR LITERATURE;
- ABILITY TO APPLY LINGUISTIC AND CULTURAL COMPETENCIES IN PROFESSIONAL SECTORS CHARACTERIZED BY INTERACTIONS WITH INTERNATIONAL CONTACTS;
- KNOWLEDGE OF THE MOST COMMON COMPUTER APPLICATIONS FOR THE DIGITIZATION AND ARCHIVING OF DOCUMENTS IN BOTH THE FIELD OF CULTURAL HERITAGE AND IN THE BUSINESS SECTOR;
- KNOWLEDGE OF THE GEO-ECONOMIC SITUATION OF THE TERRITORY AND THE VARIOUS TERRITORIAL ACTIVITIES;
- COMPETENCIES IN THE DEVELOPMENT AND COMPILATION OF EUROPEAN PROJECTS.
THESE RESULTS WILL BE ACHIEVED THROUGHOUT THE TWO-YEAR PERIOD, IN THE VARIOUS PHASES IN WHICH THE TEACHING ACTIVITY IS ORGANIZED, THROUGH LECTURES, SEMINARS, INTERNSHIPS, GROUP DISCUSSIONS, AND THE PREPARATION OF PAPERS.
KNOWLEDGE AND UNDERSTANDING IN THE VARIOUS SECTORS WILL BE VERIFIED THROUGH WRITTEN AND ORAL TESTS, DEPENDING ON THE MOST SUITABLE MODALITIES FOR THE DIFFERENT SUBJECTS TAUGHT.
II - Ability to apply knowledge and understanding: MASTER'S DEGREE GRADUATES MUST BE ABLE TO USE THE ACQUIRED KNOWLEDGE: IN THE FIELD OF LANGUAGES AND LITERATURES OF SPECIALIZATION TO MANAGE ISSUES RELATED TO LINGUISTIC EXCHANGE, THE RECEPTION AND DISSEMINATION OF TEXTS IN FOREIGN LANGUAGES, THE PRODUCTION AND UNDERSTANDING OF PAPERS OF VARIOUS NATURES AND TYPES, METALINGUISTIC REFLECTION, THE DESIGN AND IMPLEMENTATION OF PROJECTS RELATED TO TEACHING, DISSEMINATION, CULTURAL PROGRAMMING, COMMUNICATION IN ECONOMIC SECTORS CHARACTERIZED BY A HIGH LEVEL OF INTERNATIONALIZATION, AND THE PREPARATION OF EUROPEAN PROJECTS RESPONDING TO THE CULTURAL AND BUSINESS RESOURCES AND NEEDS OF THE TERRITORY.
THE EXTENSION AND DEPTH OF THE LINGUISTIC AND CULTURAL AREAS OF COMPETENCE, AS WELL AS THE STUDY OF THE VARIOUS GEO-ECONOMIC CONTEXTS, PROVIDE STUDENTS WITH MULTIPLE TOOLS FOR REFLECTION. IN ADDITION, THE ACQUISITION OF PRECISE INTERDISCIPLINARY METHODOLOGIES IN THE FIELD OF TRANSLATION, LITERARY AND PHILOLOGICAL ANALYSIS, SPECIALIZED LINGUISTIC INTERPRETATION, COMPUTER APPLICATIONS AND DIGITAL SOFTWARE, ART, MANAGEMENT ECONOMICS, AND EUROPEAN PROJECT DESIGN, WILL ALLOW STUDENTS TO ADAPT MORE EASILY TO DIFFERENT WORK CONTEXTS, FROM SCIENTIFIC-LITERARY RESEARCH TO TEACHING, TO THE VARIOUS TYPES OF BUSINESSES, DEPENDING ON THE CHOSEN CURRICULUM. MASTER'S DEGREE GRADUATES MUST THEREFORE BE ABLE TO APPLY THE PRINCIPLES AND METHODOLOGIES OF LINGUISTIC AND/OR PHILOLOGICAL AND/OR TECHNICAL-COMPUTER AND/OR ARTISTIC-CULTURAL AND/OR GEO-ECONOMIC INVESTIGATION AND INTERPRETATION TO THE MOST DIVERSE COMMUNICATION CONTEXTS, TO OTHER AREAS OF KNOWLEDGE AND SOCIAL EXPERIENCE, WITH AWARENESS OF THE CONTINUOUS DYNAMICS THAT CHARACTERIZE THEM, BUT ALSO TO ISSUES OF A MORE FRANKLY THEORETICAL NATURE, EVEN FROM THE PERSPECTIVE OF FURTHER STUDIES AND RESEARCH IN UNIVERSITY AND EXTRA-UNIVERSITY SETTINGS.
THE TEACHING MODALITIES AND TOOLS THROUGH WHICH THE EXPECTED LEARNING OUTCOMES WILL BE ACHIEVED ARE MULTIPLE AND BASED ON THE ACTIVE ROLE OF THE STUDENT AND THE INTERDISCIPLINARY INTERACTION OF THE TEACHERS, IN ORDER TO ALLOW STUDENTS TO APPLY THEIR KNOWLEDGE ALSO TO RELATED DISCIPLINARY AREAS (WITH PARTICULAR ATTENTION TO INTERCULTURAL AND TRANSCULTURAL DYNAMICS) AND TO CRITICALLY COMPARE THEMSELVES WITH OTHER SCIENTIFIC AND DISCIPLINARY PERSPECTIVES.
THE EXPECTED LEARNING OUTCOMES WILL BE ACHIEVED THROUGHOUT THE TWO-YEAR PERIOD, IN THE VARIOUS PHASES IN WHICH THE TEACHING ACTIVITY IS ORGANIZED, AND VERIFIED THROUGH FINAL EXAMS AND PERIODIC WRITTEN AND ORAL TESTS; STUDENTS WILL ALSO BE REQUIRED TO ACTIVELY PARTICIPATE IN SEMINARS ON SPECIFIC TOPICS.
Level of satisfaction of graduates and employment status of graduates: GRADUATE PROFILE AND EMPLOYMENT STATUS III - Autonomy of judgment: AT THE END OF THE COURSE OF STUDIES, THE MASTER'S DEGREE GRADUATE MUST BE ABLE TO, THANKS TO THE METHODOLOGIES LEARNED, ANALYZE AND ELABORATE ALL ASPECTS OF LINGUISTIC PRODUCTION, BOTH ORAL AND WRITTEN, LITERARY TEXTS BUT ALSO TECHNICAL AND SPECIALIST TEXTS RELATED TO THE VARIOUS KNOWLEDGES, THE VARIOUS CONTEXTS AND THE VARIOUS BRANCHES OF KNOWLEDGE. THE ABILITY TO MASTER THE ACQUIRED KNOWLEDGE WILL BE EVIDENT IN THE ABILITY TO ORGANIZE, ELABORATE, AND EVALUATE THE DATA RELATING TO BOTH THE INDIVIDUAL SUBJECTS ASSIMILATED AND THEIR COMPLEX, AS WELL AS IN THE ABILITY TO FORMULATE AUTONOMOUS JUDGMENTS AND POINTS OF VIEW ON ISSUES RELATED TO LANGUAGES AND THEIR THEORETICAL STUDY, TO THE MODERN LITERATURES AND CULTURES STUDIED IN A BROAD AND DIVERSIFIED CULTURAL HORIZON, AND TO THEIR APPLICATION IN SPECIFIC PROFESSIONAL CONTEXTS.
THIS AUTONOMY OF JUDGMENT WILL BE ACQUIRED THROUGH:
- THEORETICAL-METHODOLOGICAL LECTURES;
- SEMINAR ACTIVITIES BASED ON THE ANALYSIS AND INTERPRETATION OF TEXTS AND ON ADVANCED-LEVEL METHODOLOGICAL AND THEORETICAL ISSUES;
- PREPARATION FOR THE WRITING OF PAPERS;
- PARTICIPATION IN LABORATORY ACTIVITIES;
- PARTICIPATION IN INTERNSHIP ACTIVITIES;
- STUDY AND INTERNSHIP EXPERIENCES ABROAD;
- PREPARATION OF THE FINAL PAPER.
THE VERIFICATION OF THIS AUTONOMY OF JUDGMENT WILL BE CARRIED OUT THROUGH:
- PROGRESS TESTS AND EVALUATION TESTS, BOTH ORAL AND WRITTEN;
- FINAL EVALUATION TEST.
IV - Communication skills: REGARDLESS OF THE CHOSEN CURRICULUM, THE MASTER'S DEGREE GRADUATE MUST POSSESS EXCELLENT WRITTEN AND ORAL MASTERY OF THE FOREIGN LANGUAGES STUDIED, WHICH WILL ENABLE THEM TO COMMUNICATE AT AN ADVANCED LEVEL WITH SPEAKERS OF THE LANGUAGES AND CULTURES STUDIED IN AN EFFECTIVE AND APPROPRIATE WAY, USING LANGUAGE APPROPRIATE TO DIFFERENT TYPES OF INTERLOCUTORS, STARTING FROM AN ADEQUATE MANAGEMENT OF THE LINGUISTIC USES OF EVERYDAY LIFE UP TO USES OF INCREASING COMPLEXITY. DEPENDING ON THE CHOSEN CURRICULUM, THE GRADUATE WILL ALSO ACQUIRE SPECIFIC COMPETENCIES ACCORDING TO THE OBJECTIVES SET BY THE TRAINING COURSE. THEY WILL BE ABLE TO TAKE INTO ACCOUNT THE DIFFERENT CULTURAL CONTEXTS AND THE DIFFERENT REGISTERS OF LINGUISTIC COMMUNICATION AND TO UNDERSTAND AND TRANSLATE LITERARY TEXTS AND DOCUMENTS OF VARIOUS COMPLEXITY; THEY WILL ALSO BE ABLE TO ADEQUATELY PRESENT THE CULTURAL CONTENTS OF THE VARIOUS DISCIPLINES THROUGH THE USE OF APPROPRIATE TERMINOLOGIES AND REGISTERS IN A WIDE RANGE OF COMMUNICATION AND EXPRESSIVE SITUATIONS, AS WELL AS TO ELABORATE ARTICULATE AND RELEVANT CRITICAL ANALYSES ON THE ISSUES ADDRESSED. THEY WILL BE ABLE TO ORGANIZE LANGUAGE TRAINING COURSES FOR FOREIGNERS AND/OR FOREIGN LANGUAGE LEARNING COURSES. THEY WILL BE ABLE TO COMMUNICATE WITH FOREIGN PARTNERS IN THE VARIOUS FIELDS AND WORK CONTEXTS; THEY WILL ALSO BE ABLE TO MANAGE CULTURAL AND COMMERCIAL EXCHANGES IN AN INTERNATIONAL ECONOMIC AND BUSINESS CONTEXT; FINALLY, THEY WILL BE ABLE TO APPLY THE NEW COMPUTER METHODOLOGIES RELATED TO THE SYSTEMS FOR ARCHIVING AND DIGITIZING DOCUMENTS, BOTH IN THE FIELD OF CULTURAL HERITAGE AND IN THE BUSINESS SECTOR.
THESE COMMUNICATION/TEACHING AND MANAGEMENT SKILLS WILL BE ACQUIRED THROUGH:
- THEORETICAL LESSONS ACCOMPANIED BY APPLIED MOMENTS;
- SEMINARS AND MEETINGS FOR IN-DEPTH DISCUSSION WITH EXPERTS, BOTH ITALIAN AND FOREIGN, OF THE VARIOUS DISCIPLINES;
- PARTICIPATION IN LABORATORY ACTIVITIES;
- PARTICIPATION IN INTERNSHIP ACTIVITIES;
- STUDY AND INTERNSHIP EXPERIENCES ABROAD;
- PREPARATION OF REPORTS AND ORAL PRESENTATIONS;
- PREPARATION OF WRITTEN PAPERS IN VIEW OF THE FINAL PAPER;
- FINAL PAPER WHICH CONSTITUTES FOR THE STUDENT A FURTHER OPPORTUNITY FOR IN-DEPTH STUDY AND VERIFICATION OF THE ANALYSIS AND ELABORATION CAPABILITIES DEVELOPED DURING THE TWO-YEAR PERIOD.
THE VERIFICATION OF THESE COMMUNICATION SKILLS WILL BE CARRIED OUT THROUGH:
- PROGRESS TESTS AND EVALUATION TESTS, BOTH ORAL AND WRITTEN;
- REPORTS ON THE RESULTS OF PARTICIPATION IN SEMINAR AND LABORATORY ACTIVITIES;
- FINAL EVALUATION TEST.
V - Learning ability: GRADUATES WILL DEVELOP SPECIFIC LEARNING ABILITIES, EVEN AT HIGHLY COMPLEX LEVELS, IN THE VARIOUS AREAS PROVIDED FOR BY THE TRAINING COURSES. IN PARTICULAR, THEY WILL BE ABLE TO:
- IDENTIFY AND USE THE TOOLS FOR ANALYSIS, IN-DEPTH STUDY, AND SCIENTIFIC UPDATING FOR THE LINGUISTIC, LITERARY, AND SOCIO-ECONOMIC DISCIPLINES (EXPERT USE OF BIBLIOGRAPHIC AND MEDIA TOOLS FOR CHECKING AND VERIFYING THE KNOWLEDGE POSSESSED, AND FOR CONTINUOUS SELF-UPDATING OF SUCH KNOWLEDGE);
- ACCESS SCIENTIFIC LITERATURE PRODUCED IN AT LEAST TWO EUROPEAN LANGUAGES IN ADDITION TO THEIR OWN;
- DEVELOP AN ATTITUDE TOWARDS CRITICAL ELABORATION AND RESEARCH IN THE SPECIFIC AREAS OF COMPETENCE;
- USE NEW TEACHING AND RESEARCH TOOLS IN THE FIELD OF VARIOUS PROFESSIONAL CONTEXTS;
- USE THE MAIN COMPUTER TOOLS IN THE SPECIFIC AREAS OF COMPETENCE (SKILLS RELATED TO THE NEW LANGUAGES OF COMMUNICATION AND INFORMATION).
THESE ABILITIES AND STRATEGIES WILL ENABLE GRADUATES TO TACKLE IN A FULLY AUTONOMOUS WAY FURTHER STUDIES, BOTH IN THESE SAME SPECIFIC FIELDS AND IN OTHER RELATED DISCIPLINARY AREAS, IN THE UNIVERSITY, IN OTHER INSTITUTIONS OF HIGHER SCIENTIFIC EDUCATION, IN THE VARIOUS WORK CONTEXTS. THEY CONSTITUTE AN INDISPENSABLE PREMISE FOR ACCESS TO THE THIRD CYCLE OF ACADEMIC EDUCATION (RESEARCH DOCTORATE, SCIENTIFIC ADVANCED TRAINING COURSES, LEVEL II MASTER'S PROGRAMS), ADMISSION TO ANY SPECIALIZATION SCHOOLS, TO CONTINUOUS TRAINING UPDATE PROCESSES RELATED TO THE WORK FIELD.
THESE LEARNING ABILITIES WILL BE ACQUIRED IN THE COURSE OF STUDY AS A WHOLE, IN PARTICULAR THROUGH:
- THEORETICAL-METHODOLOGICAL AND PRACTICAL-APPLIED LESSONS;
- ADVANCED-LEVEL EXERCISES, SEMINARS, AND SPECIALIZED LABORATORY ACTIVITIES;
- PARTICIPATION IN MEETINGS AND STUDY DAYS;
- CONSULTATION OF TEXTS, TEACHING MATERIALS, AND SCIENTIFIC DOCUMENTATION;
- SPECIFIC INTERNSHIP ACTIVITIES;
- INDIVIDUAL STUDY, PREPARATION OF PROJECTS IN SEMINAR SETTINGS, AND ABOVE ALL THE ACTIVITY CARRIED OUT FOR THE PREPARATION OF THE FINAL EXAM.
THE ABILITY TO LEARN IS EVALUATED THROUGH CONTINUOUS VERIFICATION FORMS (PROGRESS TESTS AND EVALUATION TESTS, BOTH WRITTEN AND ORAL) DURING THE TRAINING ACTIVITIES, WITH PARTICULAR REGARD TO THE ABILITY TO RESPECT DEADLINES, THE COMMITMENT IN THE PRESENTATION OF DATA AUTONOMOUSLY OBTAINED, AND THE PROJECTING ABILITY DEMONSTRATED DURING THE ELABORATION OF THE FINAL EXAM.
